    GROUP PROFILE
    集團簡介



    Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”, together with               匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」，連同其附屬公
    its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a well-established supplier of customised      司統稱「本集團」）是一家信譽卓著的定制電線
    interconnect solutions with over 30 years’ experience in the industry. The       互連方案供應商 ，在行業擁有超過30年經驗 。
    Group is headquartered in Hong Kong, and has manufacturing facilities            本集團的總部設於香港 ，並於中華人民共和國
    in Shanghai, Suzhou, Jiangxi and Huizhou, the People’s Republic of China        （「中國」）上海 、蘇州 、江西及惠州 ，以及日本
    (“PRC”), Japan and Mexico.                                                       及墨西哥擁有生產設施 。

    The Group currently manufactures and supplies a wide variety of copper          本集團目前製造及供應各種銅纜和光纜電線組
    & optical fiber cable assemblies, digital cable products, medical products      件 、數字電線產品 、醫療產品及服務器 ，產
    and servers which are produced to the specifications and designs of             品均根據個別客戶夥伴的詳述規格及設計而生
    its individual customer partners. Its products are used by a number of          產 。產品已使用在包括電訊 、數據中心 、工業
    established PRC and international customers in a variety of market sectors,     設備 、醫療設備 、汽車配線 、數字電線及服務
    including telecommunication, data centre, industrial equipment, medical         器在內的各種市場領域 ，均受不少商譽優良的
    equipment, automotive wire harness, digital cable and server.                   中國及國際客戶所採用 。

    Its business features on customised cable and server products for a variety     其業務特點為定制電線及服務器產品 ，適用於
    of specifications which are used in a wide range of applications. The Group     廣泛應用中的各種規格 。本集團按合約生產服
    supplies cable and server products on a Contract Manufacturing Services         務(CMS)基準供應電線及服務器產品 ，而其電
    (CMS) basis, and its cable and server products are made-to-order, upon          線及服務器產品在收到客戶訂單後定制生產 。
    receipt of its customers’ orders.

    VISION                                                                          願景
    To be a leading provider of interconnect products, continually recognised        成為互連產品的領先供應商 ，持續被公認為
    as a “Preferred Supplier” capable of delivering world class quality, products   「優選供應商」，具備提供世界一流的品質 、產
    and services – EACH and EVERY TIME.                                              品和服務的實力 。

    STRATEGY                                                                        策略
    To become a Tier-1 supplier of cable products and high performance              成為電訊 、數據通訊 、醫療保健 、汽車及工業
    interconnect products to the market leaders in the telecommunication,           領域的市場領導者之電線產品和高性能互連產
    data communication, healthcare, automotive, and industrial sectors,             品的一級供應商 ，同時繼續擴展其產品範圍 ，
    while continuously expanding its product range to meet evolving market          以滿足不斷變化的市場需求 。
    demands.

     Gearing Ratio                                                                   負債比率
     Gearing ratio is calculated as net debt (defined as bank loans, loans           負債比率根據債務淨額（定義為銀行貸款 、來
     from parent company and lease liabilities less bank balances and cash           自母公司的貸款以及租賃負債減去銀行結餘及
     and pledged deposits) divided by the sum of net debt and total equity           現金及已抵押存款）除以債務淨額及總權益之
     and multiplied by 100%. As at 31 December 2024, the Group’s gearing             和再乘以100%計算 。於2024年12月31日 ，本
     ratio was 33.5% as compared to 55.3% in the Previous Financial Period,          集團的負債比率為33.5% ，而上一個財政期間
     the decrease was mainly attributable to the decrease of bank loans and          則為55.3% ，減少主要歸因於立訊集團為本集
     loans provided from Luxshare Group for financing the Group’s operating          團的經營營運資金提供銀行貸款及貸款均有所
     working capital.                                                                減少 。

FOREIGN EXCHANGE EXPOSURE                                                       外匯風險
Most of the Group’s receipts and payments are denominated in United             本集團大部分收付款以美元 、港元 、人民幣及
States dollars, Hong Kong dollars, Renminbi and Euro. The Group’s               歐元計值 。本集團管理層透過訂立遠期外匯合
management monitors the risk of related foreign exchange exposure               約監察其相關外匯風險 。匯率波動可能對本集
by entering into forward foreign exchange contracts. Foreign currency           團業績產生影響 。本集團管理層持續評估本集
exchange rates are volatile and may have an impact on the Group’s               團的外幣風險 ，並於必要時採取措施盡量降低
results. The Group’s management evaluates the Group’s foreign currency          本集團的風險 。
exposure on a continuing basis and takes actions to minimise the Group’s
exposure whenever necessary.

TREASURY POLICIES                                                               庫務政策

in any high-risk speculative activities. However, the Group’s management        動 。然而 ，本集團管理層會監察外匯風險 ，並
monitors foreign exchange exposure and will consider hedging significant        將於需要時考慮對沖重大外幣風險 。本集團亦
foreign currency exposure should the need arise. The Group will also            將監察及維持港元現金結餘 ，以盡量減少對可
monitor and maintain a Hong Kong dollar cash balance to minimise the            能引致匯兌虧損的不必要外匯兌換的需求 。
need for unnecessary foreign exchange conversion which may result in
exchange loss.

engaged in manufacture and sales of cable assembly, digital cable and server               事 製 造 及 銷 售 電 線 組 件 ， 數 字 電 線 及 服務器產品
products (the “Core Business”). Details of the principal activities of its subsidiaries   （「核心業務」）。其附屬公司的主要業務的詳情載於綜
are set out in Note 36 to the consolidated financial statements. There were no             合財務報表附註36。本集團於截至2024年12月31日
significant changes in the nature of the Group’s principal activities during the year      止年度的主要業務性質概無重大變動 。
ended 31 December 2024.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                           股息政策
The Board has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend Policy”) on 28 December            董事會已於2018年12月28日採納一項股息政策（「股
2018. Details of the Dividend Policy is disclosed as below.                               息政策」）。股息政策詳情如下文所披露 。

The Company adopts a general dividend policy that aims to provide shareholders of         本公司採納一般股息政策 ，旨在於任何財政年度向
the Company out of the Group’s profit attributable to shareholders in any financial       本公司股東提供本集團之股東應佔溢利 ，惟須遵守
year, subject to the criteria set out below.                                              下列標準 。

Such declaration and payment of dividends shall remain to be determined at the            有關宣派及派付股息仍由董事會酌情決定 ，並須遵
discretion of the Board and subject to all applicable requirements (including without     守香港法例第622章《公司條例》及本公司組織章程大
limitation restrictions on dividend declaration and payment) under the Companies          綱及細則的所有適用規定（包括但不限於股息宣派及
Ordinance (Cap. 622 of the Laws of Hong Kong) and the memorandum and articles             派付限制）。
of association of the Company.

In proposing any dividend payout, the Board shall also take into account, inter alia:–    建議派付任何股息時 ，董事會亦應考慮（其中包括）
                                                                                                                 ：–

•   the Group actual and expected financial performance;                                  •   本集團的實際及預期財務業績；

•   Shareholders’ interests;                                                              •   股東權益；

•   retained earnings and distributable reserves of the Company and each of the           •   本公司及本集團其他各成員公司的保留盈利及可
    other members of the Group;                                                               分配儲備；

•   the level of the Group’s debts to equity ratio, return on equity and financial        •   本集團之負債股權比率 、股本回報率 ，以及施
    covenants to which the Group is subject;                                                  加於本集團的財務限制所處之水平；

•   possible effects on the Group’s creditworthiness;                                     •   對本集團信用可靠程度之潛在影響；

•   any restrictions on payment of dividends that may be imposed by the Group’s           •   由本集團的貸款人可能施加的任何股息派付限
    lenders;                                                                                  制；

The Board and the management of the Company are committed to the maintenance          董事會及本公司管理層致力於維持良好的企業管治
of good corporate governance practices and procedures. The Company believes           常規及程序 。本公司相信 ，良好的企業管治為有效
that good corporate governance provides a framework that is essential for             管理 、健康的企業文化 、成功的業務增長及提升股
effective management, a healthy corporate culture, successful business growth         東價值提供必要的框架 。本公司企業管治原則強調
and enhancing shareholders’ value. The corporate governance principles of the         優質的董事會 、完善的內部控制 、具透明度及對全
Company emphasise a quality Board, sound internal controls, transparency and          體股東負責 。
accountability to all shareholders.

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                                 董事會多樣性政策
The Board has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity Policy”)           董事會自2018年12月28日起採納董事會多樣性政策
since 28 December 2018 which sets out the basis to achieve diversity on the Board.     （「董事會多樣性政策」），當中載有達致董事會多樣性
Details of the Board Diversity Policy is disclosed as below.                            的基準 。董事會多樣性政策詳情披露如下 。

1. Purpose                                                                             1. 目的
   The Board Diversity Policy aims to set out the approach to achieve diversity on        董事會多樣性政策旨在載列董事會為達致多樣性
   the Board.                                                                             而採取的方針 。

2. Vision                                                                              2. 願景
   The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board             本公司明白並深信董事會多樣性對提升本公司的
   to enhance the quality of its performance.                                             表現質素裨益良多 。

3. Policy                                                                              3. 政策
   When determining the composition of the Board, the Company will consider               本公司在設定董事會組成時會從多個方面考慮董
   board diversity in terms of, among other things, gender, age, experience,              事會的多樣性 ，其中包括性別 、年齡 、經驗 、
   cultural and educational background, expertise, skills and know-how. All Board         文化及教育背景 、專業經驗 、技能及知識 。董
   appointments will be based on merits, and candidates will be considered against        事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原則 ，並在考慮人
   objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity on the Board.      選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會多樣性的裨益 。

4. Measurable Objectives                                                               4. 可計量目標
   Selection of candidates for Board membership will be based on a range of               甄選董事會人選將按一系列多樣化範疇為基準 ，
   diversity perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age, experience,          包括但不限於性別 、年齡 、經驗 、文化及教育
   cultural and educational background, expertise, skills and know-how.                   背景 、專業經驗 、技能及知識 。

5. Monitoring and Reporting                                                            5. 監察及匯報

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                   風險管理及內部監控
     The Directors acknowledge that they have overall responsibility for overseeing the     董事明白彼等有全盤責任監督本公司內部監控 、財
     Company’s internal control, financial control and risk management system and shall     務監控及風險管理系統 ，並須持續監察其有效性 。

     risk management and internal control systems has been conducted by the Board at        性進行審閱 。
     least annually.

     Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material errors, losses or fraud,      本公司著力提供合理保證 ，避免出現重大錯誤 、損
     the Company has established a risk management procedures which comprised the           失或造假 ，為此已成立風險管理程序 ，包括以下步
     following steps:                                                                       驟：

     –   Identify risks: Identify major and significant risks that could affect the         –   識別風險：識別可能影響達成本集團目標之主要
         achievement of goals of the Group;                                                     及重大風險；

     –   Risk assessment: Assess and evaluate the identified risk according to its likely   –   風險評估：根據已識別風險的預料影響及出現之
         impact and the likelihood of occurrence;                                               可能性作出評估及評核；

     –   Risk mitigation: Develop effective control activities to mitigate the risks.       –   紓緩風險：策劃有效的監管活動 ，務求紓緩風
                                                                                                險。

     Risk identification and assessment is performed or updated annually, and the results   每年進行或更新風險識別及評估 ，風險評估 、評核
     of risk assessment, evaluation and mitigation of each functions or operation are       的結果及各功能或營運之紓緩措施會詳細記錄在風

     for reviews.

     The Group’s risk management and internal control systems are, however, designed        然而 ，本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之設計旨
     to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives,    在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險 ，僅可就
     and can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material            重大失實聲明或損失提供合理而非絕對保證 。
     misstatement or loss.

The Group is a reputable supplier of customised interconnect solutions         本集團是一家信譽卓著的定制電線互連方案供
with over 30 years of experience in the wire and cable industry. In recent     應商 ，在電線電纜行業擁有超過30年經驗 。本
years, the Group has expanded its business to server business. Its products    集團近年已拓展其業務至服務器業務 。產品已
are used by a number of established PRC and international customers in         使用在包括電訊 、數據中心 、工業設備 、醫療
a variety of market sectors, including telecommunication, data centre,         設備 、汽車配線 、數字電線及服務器在內的各
industrial equipment, medical equipment, automotive, digital cables and        種市場領域 ，均受不少商譽優良的中國及國際
server. The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of business       客戶所採用 。本集團致力於環境保護 、社會責
practices in relation to environmental protection, social responsibility and   任及相關管治方面維持高水準的業務常規 。為
related governance. In order to uphold shareholders’ confidence and            加強股東對本集團在達成企業可持續發展所作
understanding in the Group’s efforts in achieving corporate sustainability,    努力的信心及了解 ，本集團每年以透明及公開

     Climate Change                                                                   氣候變化
     Climate change is one of the most critical and complicated challenges            氣候變化是新世紀全球所面臨最嚴峻和複雜的
     faced globally in the new century. Global warming leads to more frequent         挑戰之一 。全球暖化導致出現更頻繁的極端天
     extreme weather conditions including changes in precipitation mode,              氣狀況 ，包括降雨模式變化 、乾旱 、水災和叢
     droughts, floods and bushfires. Rise in sea level may inundate coastal           林大火 。水位上升將可能淹沒沿海地區 。面對
     areas. Faced with all sorts of natural disaster, individuals, corporations and   各種自然災害 ，個人 、企業和政府必須立即採
     governments must take immediate actions to tackle climate change.                取行動應對氣候變化 。

     In response to the climate change related risks and opportunities that brings    為應對氣候變化給本集團運營帶來的相關風險
     to the operation of the Group, the Board at least annually holds meeting         與機遇 ，董事會至少每年舉行一次會議 ，監督
     to oversee climate-related strategies, policies, actions and disclosure, and     與氣候相關的策略 、政策 、行動及披露 ，並制
     develops the related measures responding to the identified risks, in order       定相關措施應對已識別的風險 ，以降低負面影
     to mitigate the negative impact. The measures will be reviewed regularly         響 。該等措施將會被定期審查 ，以評估其是否
     to assess their effectiveness.                                                   有效執行 。

     practices. The Group has also considered potential physical risks of climate     來實際的潛在風險 ，如風暴 、火災或熱浪 ，並

TO THE SHAREHOLDERS OF TIME INTERCONNECT TECHNOLOGY LIMITED                               致匯聚科技有限公司股東

OPINION                                                                                  意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Time Interconnect               本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計載列於第99至
Technology Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (together the “Group”)           171頁的匯聚科技有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公
set out on pages 99 to 171, which comprise the consolidated statement of financial       司（統稱為「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表 ，其中包括於
position as at 31 December 2024, and the consolidated statement of profit or             2024年12月31日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年

      3. BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                             3. 綜合財務報表編製基準及會計政策（續）
         AND ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)
         3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial statements (continued)              3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準（續）
             Basis of preparation                                                                   編製基準
             The consolidated financial statements have been prepared under the                     除若干金融工具以及租賃土地及樓宇按下文
             historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments and leasehold           所闡釋會計政策以報告期末的公平值及重估
             land and buildings, which are measured at fair values and revalued amounts,            金額計量外 ，綜合財務報表按歷史成本基礎
             at the end of the reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies            編製 。
             set out below.

             Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration given      歷史成本一般根據為交換貨品及服務而付出
             in exchange for goods and services.                                                  代價之公平值計算 。

             Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to           公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交
             transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants           易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之價
             at the measurement date, regardless of whether that price is directly                格 ，而不論該價格是否可直接觀察或使用其
             observable or estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating             他估值方法估計 。於估計資產或負債之公平
             the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the          值時 ，本集團會考慮市場參與者於計量日期
             characteristics of the asset or liability if the market participants would take      對資產或負債定價時所考慮的資產或負債特
             those characteristics into account when pricing the asset or liability at the        點 。綜合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途
             measurement date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes              之公平值乃按此基準釐定 ，惟於香港財務報
             in the consolidated financial statements is determined on such basis, except         告準則第2號「以股份為基礎付款」範圍內之
             for share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS              以股份為基礎付款的交易 、於香港財務報告
             2 “Share-based Payment”, leasing transactions that are within the scope              準則第16號「租賃」範圍內之租賃交易及與公
             of HKFRS 16 “Leases”, and measurements that have some similarities to                平值類似但並非公平值之計量（如香港會計
             fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2            準則第2號「存貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計
             “Inventories” or value in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets”.                     準則第36號「資產減值」之使用價值）除外 。

             A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account a               非金融資產的公平值計量考慮了市場參與者
             market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using the asset        以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產或將該資產
             in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market participant that      出售予將以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產的
             would use the asset in its highest and best use.                                     另一市場參與者而產生經濟效益的能力 。

             For leasehold land and building measured at revalued amounts, which are              就按公平值交易的按重估金額計量的租賃土
             transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that unobservable inputs          地及建築物及於隨後期間使用不可觀察輸入
             are to be used to measure fair value in subsequent periods, the valuation            數據計量公平值的估值方法而言 ，估值方法
             technique is calibrated so that at initial recognition the results of the            應予校準 ，以使估值方法於初步確認時的結
             valuation technique equals the transaction price.                                    果相等於交易價格 。

             In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value measurements are           此外 ，就財務報告而言 ，公平值計量根據公
             categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the degree to which the inputs             平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及公平值計
             to the fair value measurements are observable and the significance of the            量之輸入數據對其整體重要性分類為第一 、
             inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which are described as         第二或第三級 ，詳情如下：
             follows:

             •   Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets                  •   第一級輸入數據為實體於計量日期可以
                 for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the                取得的相同資產或負債在活躍市場之報
                 measurement date;                                                                    價（未經調整）
                                                                                                            ；

             •   Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included within              •   第二級輸入數據為就資產或負債直接或
                 Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly or          間接可觀察之輸入數據（第一級內包括的
                 indirectly; and                                                                      報價除外）
                                                                                                          ；及

             •   Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.               •   第三級輸入數據為資產或負債的不可觀
                                                                                                      察輸入數據 。

       Basis of consolidation                                                                   綜合基準
       The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial statements               綜合財務報表包括本公司及以及本公司及其
       of the Company and entities controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries.              附屬公司所控制的實體之財務報表 。倘屬以
       Control is achieved when the Company:                                                    下情況 ，則本公司獲得控制權：

       •   has power over the investee;                                                       •   對被投資方擁有權力；

       •   is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with           •   通過對被投資方之參與面臨可變回報之
           the investee; and                                                                      風險或取得可變回報之權利；及

       •   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                            •   有能力行使其權力以影響其回報 。

       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts and               倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控制權條件之
       circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of the three              其中一項或多項有變 ，本集團會重新評估其
       elements of control listed above.                                                      是否控制被投資方 。

       The consolidated financial statements incorporate the results of business              綜合財務報表使用收購法納入業務合併的業

       is transferred to the Group. In the statement of financial position, the               表中 ，被收購方的可識別資產 、負債及或

             Associates                                                                                聯營公司
             Where the Group has the power to participate in (but not control) the                     倘本集團有權參與（但無法控制）另一實體的
             financial and operating policy decisions of another entity, it is classified as an        財務和經營政策決策時 ，該實體歸類為聯營
             associate. Associates are initially recognised in the consolidated statement              公司 。聯營公司初步按成本於綜合財務狀況
             of financial position at cost. Subsequently, associates are accounted for                 表確認 。隨後 ，聯營公司以權益法入賬 ，而
             using the equity method, where the Group's share of post-acquisition                      本集團分佔收購後溢利及虧損及其他全面收
             profits and losses and other comprehensive income is recognised in the                    益於綜合損益及其他全面收益表確認（虧損
             consolidated statement of profit and loss and other comprehensive income                  超出本集團於聯營公司的投資除外 ，除非有
             (except for losses in excess of the Group's investment in the associate unless            責任彌補該等虧損）。
             there is an obligation to make good those losses).

             Any premium paid for an associate above the fair value of the Group's                   就聯營公司支付高於本集團應佔所收購可識
             share of the identifiable assets, liabilities and contingent liabilities acquired       別資產 、負債及或然負債公平值的任何溢價
             is capitalised and included in the carrying amount of the associate. Where              均撥充資本 ，並計入該聯營公司賬面值 。若
             there is objective evidence that the investment in an associate has been                有客觀證據顯示於聯營公司的投資已減值 ，

             the same way as other non-financial assets.                                             的方式進行減值測試 。

             Revenue from contracts with customers                                                   來自客戶合約的收益
             The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance obligation                       本集團於完成履約責任之時（或就此）確認
             is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services underlying the                收益 ，即於特定履約責任相關貨品或服務的
             particular performance obligation is transferred to the customer.                       「控制權」移交客戶時 。

             A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle of                   履約責任指特定的貨品或服務（或一組貨品
             goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or services that      或服務）或一系列大致相同的特定貨品或服
             are substantially the same.                                                             務。

             Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over time by                 如符合以下其中一項標準 ，則控制權隨時間
             reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                 轉移 ，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任
             performance obligation if one of the following criteria is met:                         的進展情況而隨時間確認：

             •    the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits                     •   本集團履約時 ，客戶同時取得並耗用本
                  provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs;                             集團履約所提供的利益；

             •    the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that the                      •   本集團的履約產生或提升一項資產 ，而
                  customer controls as the Group performs; or                                            該項資產於本集團履約時由客戶控制；
                                                                                                         或

             •    the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an alternative               •   本集團的履約並未產生令本集團有替代
                  use to the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to payment                     用途的資產 ，且本集團對迄今已完成履
                  for performance completed to date.                                                     約具有可強制執行的收取款項權利 。

             Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the customer                   否則 ，收益於客戶取得特定貨品或服務之控
             obtains control of the distinct good or service.                                        制權時確認 。

       Principal versus agent                                                               委託人對代理人
       When another party is involved in providing goods or services to a customer,         當涉及另一方向客戶提供貨品或服務時 ，本
       the Group determines whether the nature of its promise is a performance              集團釐定其承諾的性質是否為其自身提供指
       obligation to provide the specified goods or services itself (i.e. the Group is      定貨品或服務本身的履約責任（即本集團為
       a principal) or to arrange for those goods or services to be provided by the         委託人）或安排由另一方提供該等貨品或服
       other party (i.e. the Group is an agent).                                            務（即本集團為代理人）。

       For resale of server components, the Group did not control those goods               就轉售服務器部件而言 ，本集團於貨品轉讓
       before they were transferred to a customer. The Group is primarily                   至客戶前並未控制該等貨品 。本集團主要負
       responsible to arrange for the provision of the goods by another party and           責安排另一方提供貨品 ，並無權釐定價格 。
       does not have the discretion in setting the price. In this case, the Group           就此而言 ，本集團就為換取另一方安排提供
       recognises revenue in the amount of any fee or commission to which it                指定貨品預期有權取得的任何收費或佣金的
       expects to be entitled in exchange for arranging for the specified goods to          金額確認收入 。

       Contract balances                                                                    合約餘額
       A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration in exchange           合約資產指本集團就向客戶換取本集團已轉
       for goods or services that the Group has transferred to a customer that is not       讓的貨品或服務收取代價的權利（尚未成為
       yet unconditional. It is assessed for impairment on the same basis as trade          無條件）。其以與貿易應收款項相同的基準評
       receivables. In contrast, a receivable represents the Group’s unconditional          估減值 。相反 ，應收款項指本集團收取代價
       right to consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required before             的無條件權利 ，即只需待時間過去代價即須
       payment of that consideration is due.                                                到期支付 。

       A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer goods or          合約負債指本集團就已向客戶收取的代價（或
       services to a customer for which the Group has received consideration (or            代價到期的金額）而承擔向該客戶轉讓貨品
       an amount of consideration is due) from the customer.                                或服務的責任 。

       A contract asset and a contract liability relating to the same contract are          有關同一合約之合約資產及合約負債按淨額
       accounted for and presented on a net basis.                                          基準入賬及呈列 。

       Taxation                                                                              稅項
       Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently payable and                所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅項之總
       deferred tax. Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss, except        和 。當期及遞延稅項於損益確認 ，惟在其與
       when they relate to items that are recognised in other comprehensive                  於其他全面收入或直接於權益內確認的項目
       income or directly in equity, in which case, the current and deferred tax             有關的情況下 ，當期及遞延稅項亦會分別於
       are also recognised in other comprehensive income or directly in equity               其他全面收入或直接於權益內確認 。
       respectively.

       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year. Taxable            當期應付稅項乃根據年內的應課稅溢利計
       profit differs from profit before taxation because it excludes items of               量 。應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不同 ，乃由
       income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and it                於不包括其他年度的應課稅收入或可扣稅開
       further excludes items that are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s              支項目 ，且不包括毋須課稅或不可扣稅的項
       liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted        目 。本集團即期稅項負債以報告期末前已實
       or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                          施或實質上已實施之稅率計算 。

       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences. Deferred tax liabilities         遞延稅項按暫時差額而確認 。遞延稅項負債
       are generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax          通常會就所有應課稅暫時差額確認 。遞延稅
       assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary differences and          項資產通常會就所有可扣稅暫時差額及未動
       unused tax losses to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be      用稅項虧損予以確認 ，但僅限於應課稅溢利
       available against which those deductible temporary differences and unused             很可能將用以抵銷該等可動用的可扣稅暫時
       tax losses can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities are not recognised if         差額及未動用稅項虧損 。倘不屬於業務合併
       the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of assets and            的交易並不影響應課稅溢利及會計溢利 ，亦
       liabilities in a transaction that is not a business combination, affects neither      不會產生同等應課稅及可扣稅暫時差額 ，而
       the taxable profit nor the accounting profit and does not give rise to equal          初始確認該交易的資產及負債會產生暫時差
       taxable and deductible temporary difference.                                          額 ，則該等資產及負債將不予確認 。

             case the increase is credited to profit or loss to the extent of the decrease        益 ，惟以先前扣除的減幅為限 。重估使用權
             previously charged. A decrease in net carrying amount arising on revaluation         資產產生的賬面淨值減少於損益確認 ，惟以
             of right-of-use asset is recognised in profit or loss to the extent that it          超出過往重估該資產的物業重估儲備結餘（如

             a previous revaluation of that asset. On the subsequent sale or retirement           將應佔重估盈餘轉入累計溢利 。
             of a revalued asset, the attributable revaluation surplus is transferred to

             units.

             are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that              流量按稅前折現率折現至其現值 。該折現率
             reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the                反映現時市場對貨幣時間值及未調整未來現
             risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating unit) for which the estimates       金流量估計的資產（或現金產生單位）特定風
             of future cash flows have not been adjusted.                                          險的評估 。

             cash-generating unit) is reduced to its recoverable amount. An impairment             面值扣減至其可收回金額 。減值虧損即時於

                 In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                   於評估信貸風險自初始確認以來有否大
                 initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring                  幅增加時 ，本集團比較金融工具於報告
                 on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk                       日期出現違約的風險與該金融工具於初
                 of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                     始確認日期出現違約的風險 。作此評估
                 initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group considers                      時 ，本集團會考慮合理並有理據支持的
                 both quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable and                     定量及定性資料 ，包括過往經驗及毋須
                 supportable, including historical experience and forward-looking                         花費不必要成本或努力即可獲得的前瞻
                 information that is available without undue cost or effort.                              性資料 。

                 In particular, the following information is taken into account when                  尤其是 ，評估信貸風險有否大幅增加時
                 assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                           會考慮下列資料：

                 •    an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial                •   金融工具外（如有）或內部信貸評級
                      instrument’s external (if available) or internal credit rating;                     的實際或預期重大惡化；

                 •    significant deterioration in external market indicators of credit risk,         •   信貸風險的外部市場指標的重大惡
                      e.g. a significant increase in the credit spread, the credit default                化 ，例如信貸息差大幅增加 ，債務
                      swap prices for the debtor;                                                         人的信貸違約掉期價大幅上升；

                 •    existing or forecast adverse changes in business, financial or                  •   業務 、財務或經濟狀況的現有或預
                      economic conditions that are expected to cause a significant                        測不利變動 ，預期將導致債務人履
                      decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations;                      行其債務責任的能力大幅下降；

                 •    an actual or expected significant deterioration in the operating                •   債務人經營業績的實際或預期重大
                      results of the debtor; and                                                          惡化；及

                 •    an actual or expected significant adverse change in the regulatory,             •   債務人監管 、經濟或技術環境的實
                      economic, or technological environment of the debtor that results                   際或預期重大不利變動 ，導致債務
                      in a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt                  人履行其債務責任的能力大幅下降 。
                      obligations.

                 Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment, the Group                       不論上述評估結果 ，本集團假定 ，如合
                 presumes that the credit risk has increased significantly since initial              約付款逾期未付超過30天 ，則信貸風險
                 recognition when contractual payments are more than 30 days past                     自初始確認以來已大幅增加 ，惟本集團
                 due, unless the Group has reasonable and supportable information that                擁有合理並有理據支持的資料顯示情況
                 demonstrates otherwise.                                                              並非如此 ，則作別論 。

                 The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria used to               本集團定期監察用以確定信貸風險曾否
                 identify whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk and            顯著增加的標準的成效 ，並於適當時候
                 revises them as appropriate to ensure that the criteria are capable of               作出修訂 ，從而確保有關標準能夠於款
                 identifying significant increase in credit risk before the amount becomes            項逾期前確定信貸風險顯著增加 。
                 past due.

             (ii) Definition of default                                                             (ii) 違約的定義
                  For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an event of                   就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認
                  default occurs when information developed internally or obtained from                  為 ，倘內部生成或自外部來源獲得的資
                  external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors,           料顯示債務人不太可能向其債權人（包括
                  including the Group, in full.                                                          本集團）悉數付款 ，則發生違約事件 。

                 Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default has occurred             不論上述情況如何 ，本集團認為 ，倘金
                 when a financial asset is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                融資產逾期超過90天時則發生違約 ，惟
                 has reasonable and supportable information to demonstrate that a                     本集團擁有合理並有理據支持的資料證
                 more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                                  明較寬鬆的違約標準屬更合適 ，則作別
                                                                                                      論。

       (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                                  (iii) 金融資產信貸減值
             A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of default                 當發生一項或多項對金融資產估計未來
             that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of                    現金流量有不利影響的違約事件時 ，金
             that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                  融資產出現信貸減值 。金融資產信貸減
             credit-impaired includes observable data about the following events:                    值的證據包括以下可觀察事件：

           (a) significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;                  (a) 發行人或借款人出現重大財政困難；

           (b) a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                       (b) 違約 ，例如拖賬或逾期事項；

           (c) the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons               (c) 借款人的放款人因與借款人出現財
               relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the               務困難有關之經濟或合約理由而給
               borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                      予借款人在其他情況下放款人不予
               consider; or                                                                         考慮之優惠條件；或

           (d) it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter bankruptcy or               (d) 借款人有可能破產或進行其他財務
               other financial reorganisation.                                                      重組 。

       (iv) Write-off policy                                                                 (iv) 撇銷政策
            The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information                      倘有資料顯示交易對手方陷入嚴重財務
            indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty and                困難且無實際可收回之期望（如交易對手
            there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example, when the                     方已進行清盤或進入破產程序 ，或如屬
            counterparty has been placed under liquidation or has entered into                    貿易應收款項 ，該款項已逾期超過一年）
            bankruptcy proceedings, or in the case of trade receivables, when the                （以較早發生者為準），本集團則撇銷金
            amounts are over one year past due, whichever occurs sooner. Financial                融資產 。經考慮法律意見（如適用）後 ，
            assets written off may still be subject to enforcement activities under               已撇銷之金融資產仍可能受制於本集團
            the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice                     收回程序下之執法活動 。撇銷構成取消
            where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event. Any                 確認事件 。其後的任何收回均在損益確
            subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.                               認。

       (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                                (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
           The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default, loss              預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概率 、
           given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and              違約損失率（即違約時的損失程度）及違
           the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability of default                 約風險的函數 。評估違約概率及違約損
           and loss given default is based on historical data and forward-looking                失率的依據是過往數據及前瞻性資料 。
           information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                  預期信貸虧損的估計反映不偏不倚及概
           weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks of default               率加權金額 ，以各自發生違約的風險為
           occurring as the weights.                                                             權重釐定 。

           Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual cash flows              一般而言 ，預期信貸虧損為根據合約應
           that are due to the Group in accordance with the contract and the cash               付本集團的所有訂約現金流量與本集團
           flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                 預計收取的現金流量（按於初始確認時釐
           interest rate determined at initial recognition.                                     定的實際利率折現）之間的差額 。

           Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying amount of the              利息收入根據金融資產的總賬面值計
           financial asset unless the financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case         算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況除
           interest income is calculated based on amortised cost of the financial               外 ，於此情況下 ，利息收入根據金融資
           asset.                                                                               產的攤銷成本計算 。

           The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit or loss for                本集團透過調整所有金融工具的賬面值
           all financial instruments by adjusting their carrying amount, with the               於損益確認其減值收益或虧損 ，惟貿易
           exception of trade receivables where the corresponding adjustment is                 應收款項除外 ，其相應調整乃透過虧損
           recognised through a loss allowance account.                                         撥備賬確認 。

         Assessment of the net realisable value of inventories                                存貨可變現淨值的評估

         management of the Group is required to exercise judgement in estimating the          存貨賬齡分析及存貨近期或其後的使用或銷售 ，
         net realisable value with reference to the ageing analysis of inventories and        本集團管理層估計可變現淨值時須行使判斷 。倘
         recent or subsequent usages or sales of inventories. Where the expectation on        若預計可變現淨值低於若干項目的成本 ，可能出
         net realisable value is lower than the cost for certain items, a write down of       現存貨撇減 。
         inventories may arise.

         to allocate resources to segments and to assess their performance.                   及評估其表現而定期審閱有關本集團組成部分的
                                                                                              內部報告識別 。

      21. CONTRACT ASSETS                                                                    21. 合約資產
         Contract assets arising from sales of cable assembly to a customer primarily          向客戶銷售電線組件而產生的合約資產主要指本
         relate to the Group’s right to consideration for work completed and not billed        集團收取已竣工而尚未收款的工程代價的權利 ，
         because the rights are condition on the Group’s future performance. Contract          原因是該等權利乃以本集團未來表現為條件 。合
         assets are transferred to trade receivables when the production of cable assembly     約資產於電線組件產品生產完成時（即於該等權
         products are completed, being when the rights become unconditional.                   利成為無條件時）轉撥至貿易應收款項 。

         The significant increase in contract assets for the year ended 31 December            截至2024年12月31日止年度 ，合約資產之大幅

      33. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                                               33. 退休福利計劃

         The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for all qualifying                      本集團為香港的所有合資格僱員參與強制性公積
         employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the scheme are held separately                         金計劃 。有關計劃的資產乃與本集團的資產分開
         from those of the Group, in funds under the control of trustees. The Group                   持有 ，並存置於受託人所控制的基金 。本集團
         contributes 5% of the relevant payroll costs to the scheme, subject to a cap                 向該計劃支付相關入息的5%作為供款 ，而上限
         of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000 of employees, which contribution                     為僱員每月有關入息30,000港元 ，有關供款乃與
         is matched by the employees. The Group also makes voluntary contribution in                  僱員的供款一致 。除強制性供款外 ，本集團亦
         addition to the mandatory contribution.                                                      作出自願供款 。

         The employees of the Group’s subsidiaries in the PRC are members of a state-                 本集團於中國附屬公司的僱員均為中國政府所營
         managed retirement benefits scheme operated by the government of the PRC.                    辦的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員 。附屬公司須
         The subsidiary is required to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to          按工資成本的指定百分比向退休福利計劃供款以
         the retirement benefits scheme to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the              為有關福利撥資 。本集團在退休福利計劃下的唯
         Group with respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the specified                一責任為作出指定供款 。
         contributions.

         The total expenses recognised in profit or loss of HK$84,744,000 (nine months                於截至2024年12月31日止年度 ，本集團按該等
         ended 31 December 2023: HK$63,980,000) for the year ended 31 December                        計劃的規定列明的比率向此等計劃作出已付及應
         2024 represent contributions paid and payable to these plans by the Group at                 付的供款於損益確認的開支總額為84,744,000港

      37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                              37. 資本風險管理
         The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will be able         為確保本集團旗下實體能夠可持續經營 ，本集團
         to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to stakeholders              對其資本進行管理 ，並透過使債務及權益達致最

         strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.                                         略於整個年度內維持不變 。

      40. ACQUISITION OF A SUBSIDIARY                                                          40. 收購一間附屬公司
         On 17 July 2024, a wholly-owned subsidiary, Time Interconnect (HK) Limited              於2024年7月17日 ，全資附屬公司匯聚工業（香
         (“Time HK”), entered into a capital increase agreement with Cosmic M.E.                 港）有限公司（「匯聚香港」）與Cosmic M.E. Inc.
         Inc. (“CME”), a limited liability company established in Japan, to inject              （「CME」，一間於日本成立之有限公司）訂立增
         JPY80,000,000 (equivalent to approximately HK$3,922,000) in cash as capital             資協議 ，以現金注資方式向CME注入80,000,000
         contribution into CME, accordingly the Group held 71.43% of equity interest             日圓（相當於約3,922,000港元），本集團因而持
         in CME. Completion of the capital injection took place on 8 July 2024 and the           有CME 71.43%股權 。注資已於2024年7月8日
         acquisition was satisfied by the issue of 500 new shares of the Company. The            完成 ，而收購事項以發行500股本公司新股份償
         principal activity of CME is provision of developing, manufacturing and selling         付 。CME的主要業務為開發 、生產及銷售電子
         of electronic medical instruments and other medical equipment and devices.              醫療儀器及其他醫療設備和器材 。

         Upon completion of the acquisition, the Group is regarded as having control             完成收購事項後 ，本集團被視為已獲得CME的控
         over CME. As such, CME is accounted for as non-wholly owned subsidiary of               制權 。因此 ，CME作為本公司的非全資附屬公
         the Company, and the financial results of CME is consolidated into the results          司入賬 ，而CME的財務業績綜合計入本集團的業
         of the Group. A gain on bargain purchase of approximately HK$397,000 was                績內 。於綜合全面收益表中錄得約397,000港元
         recorded in the consolidated statement of comprehensive income, as a result             的議價購買收益 ，乃由於已付及應付代價的公平
         of the difference between the fair value of the consideration paid and payable,         值與已收購資產淨值的公平值（即已收購可識別
         and the fair value of the net assets acquired, which are the fair value of the          資產及所承擔負債相對於其價值的公平值 ，並參
         identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed to their values with reference     考與本集團並無關連的獨立合資格估值師永利行
         to the valuation report prepared by RHL Appraisal Limited, an independent               評值顧問有限公司出具的估值報告）之間存在差
         qualified professional valuer not connected to the Group.                               額。

         Acquisition-related cost to HK$2,522,000 have been excluded from the                    收購事項相關成本約2,522,000港元並無計入已
         consideration transferred and have been recognised as an expense in the                 轉撥代價 ，並已確認為本年度開支及計入綜合損
